Main Entry Points\n\n**Primary Entry Point:** `/workspace/pkg/cmd/grafana/main.go`\n\nGrafana uses the **urfave/cli v2** framework for its CLI interface. The main entry point creates a CLI application with two primary commands:\n- `grafana-cli`: CLI utilities for plugin management and administration\n- `server`: The main HTTP server command\n\nThe server bootstrap begins in `/workspace/pkg/cmd/grafana-server/commands/cli.go` with the `RunServer()` function, which:\n1. Initializes logging and profiling (if enabled)\n2. Loads configuration from `/workspace/pkg/setting` (via `setting.NewCfgFromArgs()`)\n3. Calls `server.Initialize()` from `/workspace/pkg/server/wire.go` which uses **Google Wire** for dependency injection\n4. Starts the HTTP server and listens for system signals (SIGTERM, SIGINT)\n\nKey bootstrap services initialized through Wire:\n- **HTTP Server** (`/workspace/pkg/api/http_server.go`): Routes setup via `/workspace/pkg/api/routing`\n- **Provisioning Service** (`/workspace/pkg/services/provisioning/provisioning.go`): Loads dashboards, datasources, and alerting configs\n- **Plugin Manager** (`/workspace/pkg/plugins/manager`): Discovers and loads plugins\n- **Authentication & Authorization**: Services in `/workspace/pkg/services/auth`, `/workspace/pkg/services/authn`, `/workspace/pkg/services/accesscontrol`\n- **Database Layer** (`/workspace/pkg/infra/db`): SQL Store for persisting data\n\n---\n\n## 2. Core Backend Packages\n\n**Key Packages under `/workspace/pkg/`:**\n\n### **a) `/workspace/pkg/api/`**\nHandles HTTP API routing and REST endpoints. Contains handlers for:\n- Dashboards (`dashboard.go`)\n- Datasources (`datasources.go`)\n- Users and organizations (`user.go`, `org.go`)\n- Authentication (`login.go`)\n- Plugin management (`plugins.go`)\n\nThe `http_server.go` bootstraps the HTTP server and registers all routes via the routing system.\n\n### **b) `/workspace/pkg/services/datasources/`**\n**Datasource Service**: Manages datasource lifecycle (create, read, update, delete), caching, and access control. Key interfaces:\n- `DataSourceService`: Main service interface\n- `CacheService`: In-memory caching of datasources for performance\n\n### **c) `/workspace/pkg/services/datasourceproxy/`**\n**Data Source Proxy Service** (`datasourceproxy.go`): Routes data source queries to backend plugins or external systems. Implements:\n- `ProxyDataSourceRequest()`: Routes requests to specific datasources\n- OAuth token refresh for external services\n- Request validation and permission checks\n\n### **d) `/workspace/pkg/services/alerting/` and `/workspace/pkg/services/ngalert/`**\n**Alerting Services**: Legacy alerting (`alerting`) and new unified alerting (`ngalert`). Key responsibilities:\n- Rule evaluation and state management\n- Notification routing and delivery\n- Alert instance tracking\n- Provisioning support via `/workspace/pkg/services/provisioning/alerting/`\n\n### **e) `/workspace/pkg/services/auth/` and `/workspace/pkg/services/authn/`**\n**Authentication Services**:\n- `auth/`: Legacy authentication handlers (basic auth, OAuth, SAML, LDAP)\n- `authn/`: New authentication interface and identity management\n- `jwt/`: JWT token generation and validation\n- Integration with `/workspace/pkg/login/` for social login connectors\n\n### **f) `/workspace/pkg/services/provisioning/`**\n**Provisioning Service**: Loads configuration from YAML files and syncs to database:\n- **Dashboards** (`dashboards/`): Reads dashboard JSON from files, watches for changes, persists to DB\n- **Datasources** (`datasources/`): Configures datasources declaratively\n- **Plugins** (`plugins/`): Enables/disables plugins via configuration\n- **Alerting** (`alerting/`): Manages alert rules and notification policies\n\n---\n\n## 3. Datasource Plugin Architecture\n\n### **How Plugins Work**\n\nPlugins in Grafana communicate via **gRPC** (for backend plugins) or **direct HTTP/module loading** (for frontend plugins).\n\n**Key Directories:**\n- **Built-in datasources**: `/workspace/public/app/plugins/datasource/` (Prometheus, Loki, CloudWatch, etc.)\n- **Built-in panels**: `/workspace/public/app/plugins/panel/` (Gauge, TimeSeries, Heatmap, etc.)\n- **Plugin manager**: `/workspace/pkg/plugins/manager/` (discovery, loading, lifecycle)\n- **Backend plugin communication**: `/workspace/pkg/plugins/backendplugin/grpcplugin/` (gRPC client)\n\n### **Plugin Discovery and Loading**\n\n1. **Plugin Discovery** (`/workspace/pkg/plugins/plugins.go`):\n   - Reads `plugin.json` from each plugin directory\n   - Validates plugin.json structure (requires `type` and `id` fields)\n   - Parses plugin metadata (name, description, version, capabilities)\n\n2. **Backend Plugin Communication** (`/workspace/pkg/plugins/backendplugin/grpcplugin/`):\n   - Datasource plugins are backend processes launched via gRPC\n   - Protocol: Uses `grafana-plugin-sdk-go/backend` protocol\n   - Handles plugin lifecycle: startup, query execution, health checks\n   - Client interface in `client.go` and `client_v2.go` (v2 is newer)\n\n3. **Plugin Registry** (`/workspace/pkg/plugins/manager/registry/`):\n   - Maintains in-memory registry of loaded plugins\n   - Plugins registered by type (datasource, panel, app)\n   - Used for runtime plugin lookup\n\n### **Frontend Plugin Integration**\n\nFrontend plugins (panels, datasources) are bundled into the React app:\n- Entry point: `/workspace/public/app/index.ts`\n- Panel plugins define module pattern: `new PanelPlugin<Options>(Component)` (see `/workspace/public/app/plugins/panel/gauge/module.tsx`)\n- Plugin registry scans `/workspace/public/app/plugins/` at build time\n- Frontend uses `@grafana/data` and `@grafana/ui` packages for plugin APIs\n\n### **grafana-plugin-sdk-go Role**\n\nExternal package imported in `/workspace/pkg/plugins/backendplugin/grpcplugin/client.go`:\n- Provides gRPC service stubs for plugin-to-server communication\n- Defines backend plugin interfaces: `QueryDataHandler`, `CallResourceHandler`, `CheckHealthHandler`\n- Used by third-party datasource plugins (CloudWatch, Azure Monitor, etc.) to implement backend logic\n\n---\n\n## 4. Dashboard Provisioning Pipeline\n\n### **Configuration Reading**\n\n1. **Provisioning Path**: `/workspace/pkg/services/provisioning/dashboards/config_reader.go`\n   - Reads from `cfg.ProvisioningPath` (default: `conf/provisioning/`)\n   - Looks for YAML files in `dashboards/` subdirectory\n\n2. **YAML Format**: Example structure:\n   ```yaml\n   providers:\n     - name: 'example provider'\n       type: file\n       updateIntervalSeconds: 10\n       options:\n         path: /var/lib/grafana/dashboards\n   ```\n\n### **Dashboard Loading Flow**\n\n1. **Initialization** (`/workspace/pkg/services/provisioning/provisioning.go`, line 90-98):\n   ```\n   - `ProvisioningService.setDashboardProvisioner()` creates a new provisioner\n   - Calls `dashboards.New()` with provisioning config path\n   ```\n\n2. **File Reading** (`/workspace/pkg/services/provisioning/dashboards/file_reader.go`):\n   - `FileReader.walkDisk(ctx)` scans the filesystem for dashboard JSON files\n   - Reads dashboard JSON and parses into in-memory representation\n\n3. **Provisioning to Database** (`/workspace/pkg/services/provisioning/dashboards/dashboard.go`, line 69-87):\n   ```\n   - `Provisioner.Provision(ctx)` iterates file readers\n   - Each reader walks disk, finds dashboard files\n   - Calls provisioning service to persist dashboards\n   ```\n\n4. **Persistence** (`/workspace/pkg/services/dashboards/service/dashboard.go`):\n   - `DashboardProvisioningService.SaveProvisionedDashboard()` saves or updates dashboard in database\n   - Sets `provisioned` flag on dashboard to prevent manual UI edits\n   - Tracks provisioning metadata (source, last updated)\n\n### **Key Behavior**\n\n- **Polling Changes**: `PollChanges(ctx)` watches for file modifications\n- **Orphan Cleanup**: `CleanUpOrphanedDashboards(ctx)` deletes dashboards from provisioning sources that are no longer present\n- **Immutability**: Provisioned dashboards cannot be edited via UI by default (controlled by `GetAllowUIUpdatesFromConfig()`)\n\n---\n\n## 5. Frontend Build System\n\n### **Technology Stack**\n\n- **Framework**: React with TypeScript\n- **Build Tool**: **Webpack** (`/workspace/scripts/webpack/`)\n- **Monorepo Tool**: **NX** (near package.json scripts)\n- **Package Manager**: Yarn + Lerna\n- **Module Bundler**: Webpack with esbuild-loader for TypeScript transpilation\n\n### **Frontend Organization**\n\n**Main Packages** (`/workspace/packages/`):\n- `grafana-ui`: Core UI component library (React components)\n- `grafana-data`: Data structures and utilities (DataFrame, Query, etc.)\n- `grafana-runtime`: Runtime and plugin API definitions\n- `grafana-schema`: CUE schemas for dashboard/panel configuration\n- `grafana-prometheus`: Prometheus-specific utilities\n- `grafana-icons`: SVG icons library\n\n**Main App** (`/workspace/public/app/`):\n- `index.ts`: Frontend entry point\n- `app.ts`: App initialization and bootstrapping\n- `AppWrapper.tsx`: Root React component\n- `core/`: Core modules (navigation, services, context)\n- `features/`: Feature-specific code (dashboards, users, etc.)\n- `angular/`: Legacy Angular.js module integration\n- Plugins loaded from `/workspace/public/app/plugins/datasource/` and `/workspace/public/app/plugins/panel/`\n\n### **Build Configuration**\n\n**Entry Points** (`/workspace/scripts/webpack/webpack.prod.js`):\n- Dark theme CSS: `public/sass/grafana.dark.scss`\n- Light theme CSS: `public/sass/grafana.light.scss`\n- JavaScript module bundles assembled from TypeScript source\n\n**Output**:\n- Manifest files: `manifest.json`, `webpack-assets-manifest.json`\n- Builds output to `/workspace/public/dist/` (at runtime; not in repo)\n- CSS and JS bundles with content hashing for cache busting\n\n**Build Commands**:\n- `yarn build`: Production build (minified)\n- `yarn dev`: Development build (no minification, watch mode)\n- `yarn start`: Development server with hot reload\n\n---\n\n## 6. Extension: Adding a New Panel Plugin\n\n### **Steps to Add a Custom Visualization Panel**\n\n1. **Create Plugin Directory**:\n   ```\n   /workspace/public/app/plugins/panel/my-custom-panel/\n   ```\n\n2. **Create plugin.json**:\n   ```json\n   {\n     \"type\": \"panel\",\n     \"name\": \"My Custom Panel\",\n     \"id\": \"my-custom-panel\",\n     \"info\": {\n       \"description\": \"Custom visualization\",\n       \"author\": { \"name\": \"Your Name\" }\n     }\n   }\n   ```\n\n3. **Create module.tsx** (see `/workspace/public/app/plugins/panel/gauge/module.tsx` for example):\n   ```typescript\n   import { PanelPlugin } from '@grafana/data';\n\n   export const plugin = new PanelPlugin<Options>(MyCustomPanel)\n     .setPanelOptions(builder => {\n       builder.addStringInput({\n         path: 'myOption',\n         name: 'My Option',\n       });\n     });\n   ```\n\n4. **Create Component** (e.g., `MyCustomPanel.tsx`):\n   - React component receiving `props: PanelProps<Options>`\n   - Access data via `props.data.series`\n   - Configure via `props.options`\n\n5. **Create panelcfg.cue** (Cue schema):\n   - Defines panel options schema for UI generation\n   - Referenced by code generator to create TypeScript types\n\n6. **Create panelcfg.gen.ts** (generated types):\n   - Auto-generated from `.cue` file\n   - Provides type-safe options object\n\n### **Plugin Registry and Discovery**\n\n**How Grafana Finds New Panels**:\n1. Build time: Webpack scans `/workspace/public/app/plugins/panel/` for subdirectories\n2. Each directory with `plugin.json` is identified as a plugin\n3. `module.tsx` export is registered in the plugin system\n4. Frontend plugin registry loads and indexes plugins\n5. UI can then instantiate panels via `PanelPluginMeta`\n\n**Key Files for Panel Integration**:\n- Plugin metadata: `plugin.json` (required)\n- Plugin code: `module.tsx` (exports `plugin` variable)\n- Component implementation: `*Panel.tsx`\n- Configuration schema: `panelcfg.cue` and `panelcfg.gen.ts`\n\n**Built-in Panel Examples**:\n- Gauge: `/workspace/public/app/plugins/panel/gauge/`\n- TimeSeries: `/workspace/public/app/plugins/panel/timeseries/`\n- Heatmap: `/workspace/public/app/plugins/panel/heatmap/`\n\n---\n\n## Summary Architecture\n\n```\nGrafana Server Startup\n  ├─ CLI Entry (urfave/cli) → /pkg/cmd/grafana/main.go\n  ├─ Config Loading → /pkg/setting\n  ├─ Wire DI Setup → /pkg/server/wire.go\n  │   ├─ HTTP Server → /pkg/api/http_server.go\n  │   ├─ Plugin Manager → /pkg/plugins/manager\n  │   ├─ Provisioning Service → /pkg/services/provisioning\n  │   ├─ Datasource Service → /pkg/services/datasources\n  │   ├─ Authentication → /pkg/services/auth, /pkg/services/authn\n  │   └─ Database Layer → /pkg/infra/db\n  └─ Run Server\n      ├─ Start Background Services\n      ├─ Listen for System Signals\n      └─ HTTP Request Handler Loop\n\nFrontend Build\n  ├─ Entry Point → /public/app/index.ts\n  ├─ Webpack Config → /scripts/webpack/webpack.*.js\n  ├─ Package System → NX + Yarn\n  ├─ Plugin Discovery → /public/app/plugins/(datasource|panel)\n  └─ Build Output\n      ├─ Bundles with hashing\n      ├─ CSS (dark/light themes)\n      └─ Manifest for asset loading\n```\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":9,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2096,"cache_read_input_tokens":63058,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2096,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"52f0e635-bbc9-4339-8ac9-9ab867da181f","uuid":"3210b674-1650-4c05-a9bb-e8cacfac52eb"}
